Peak receiver throughput observed from the archived curves was approximately 19.7~Mbit/s in 2d1g, 17.9~Mbit/s in 3d1g, 16.1~Mbit/s in 4d2g, and 16.0~Mbit/s in 5d3g. Packet loss increased with forwarding complexity, reaching about 19--20\% only in the highest-load region of the three-group case.
  Conclusion: The contribution is an implementable software architecture and a feasibility study showing that stock-Android multi-group Wi-Fi Direct communication can be engineered at application level on non-rooted devices. The results support architectural feasibility in a small static testbed; they do not establish broad resilience, scalability, or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [197] [Incentivising green video streaming through a 2-tier subscription model with carbon-aware rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07910)
*Vasilios A. Siris,Adamantia Stamou,George D. Stamoulis,Konstantinos Varsos*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视频流的碳减排激励机制，通过包含折扣和碳奖励的实用两级订阅模型，激励具有环保意识的用户接受较低画质，从而降低视频流传输过程中的碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流传输消耗大量能源并产生碳排放，针对质量敏感型和环保意识型两类用户，探索根据端到端传输路径的能耗、碳强度和用户类型来制定减排激励机制的可行性，旨在通过激励用户降低画质来减少碳排放。

Method: 提出一种实用的两级订阅模型，包含折扣和碳奖励机制，允许服务提供商在特定时间段（如一个月）内将一定比例的视频以较低画质传输。通过分析视频从本地或远程数据中心传输的场景，研究碳强度、能耗和用户环保意识对激励设计和最大低画质视频比例的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（1）降低画质层级设置为比用户满意所需分辨率低一级的订阅方案最受欢迎；（2）当视频从本地数据中心传输时，低画质视频的最大比例仅取决于碳强度和平均强度上限，而激励力度还取决于用户的环保意识；（3）当视频可从碳强度不同的本地或远程数据中心传输时，低画质视频的最大比例和激励取决于数据中心的相对碳强度和能耗，以及远程数据中心额外的网络能耗成本。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于碳强度、能耗和用户类型的两级订阅激励机制能有效促进视频流的碳减排，为视频流服务提供商设计环保订阅方案提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: We investigate incentives for reducing the carbon emissions of video streaming that depend on the energy consumption of segments in the end-to-end video delivery path, the carbon intensity, and the user type, i.e., quality-sensitive and green or environmentally conscious users. The incentives can be offered through a practical 2-tier subscription model with a discount and carbon rewards, which gives providers the flexibility to reduce the quality for up to a maximum percentage of videos within a time period, such as one month. The key features of our approach are i) it is preferable to offer subscriptions where the reduced-quality tier is set one resolution level below the resolution required for maximum user satisfaction; ii) when a video is streamed from a local data center, the maximum percentage of videos streamed at a lower quality depends solely on the carbon intensity and the average intensity cap, whereas the incentives also depend on the users' level of environmental consciousness; iii) when a video can be streamed from a local or a remote data center with different carbon intensities, the maximum percentage of videos streamed at lower quality and the incentives depend on the relative carbon intensity and energy consumption at the data centers, and the additional network energy costs from the remote data center.

</details>


### [198] [Beyond Static Forecasting: Unleashing the Power of World Models for Mobile Traffic Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08199)
*Xiaoqian Qi,Haoye Chai,Yue Wang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobiWM，一种面向移动网络的世界模型，通过融合多模态环境上下文和网络参数动作，实现了高保真的移动流量预测和无限视距的网络调整反事实仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的移动流量预测模型主要关注学习静态的长期时间模式，难以捕捉移动流量与网络参数调整（如功率、方位角、机械倾角、电倾角）之间的动态关系，这限制了网络规划和优化的效果。

Method: 提出MobiWM世界模型，将移动流量作为系统状态，通过预测骨干网络建模状态与网络参数动作之间的动态关系；融合图像和序列数据等多模态环境上下文与编码动作，利用共享空间语义增强空间理解能力。

Result: 在覆盖9个区域31,900个小区的可变参数移动流量数据上，MobiWM在所有评估场景中均实现了最佳分布保真度，显著优于现有流量预测基线和代表性世界模型。

Conclusion: MobiWM成功构建了面向移动网络的世界模型，支持无限视距的连续网络调整动作轨迹推演，为运营商提供了可探索的反事实仿真环境，基于RL的下游案例研究进一步验证了其作为网络优化仿真环境的有效性，建立了数字孪生驱动的无线网络管理新范式。

Abstract: Mobile traffic prediction is a fundamental yet challenging problem for wireless network planning and optimization. Existing models focus on learning static long-term temporal patterns in mobile traffic series, which limits their ability to capture the dynamics between mobile traffic and network parameter adjustments. In this paper, we propose MobiWM, a world model for mobile networks. Taking mobile traffic as the system state, MobiWM models the dynamics between the states and network parameter actions, including power, azimuth, mechanical tilt, and electrical tilt through a predictive backbone. It fuses multimodal environmental contexts, comprising both image and sequential data, with encoded actions, leveraging shared spatial semantics to enhance spatial understanding. Leveraging the capacity of world models to capture real-world operational dynamics, MobiWM supports unlimited-horizon rollout over continuous network-adjustment action trajectories, providing operators with an explorable counterfactual simulation environment for network planning and optimization. Extensive experiments on variable-parameter mobile traffic data covering 31,900 cells across 9 districts demonstrate that MobiWM achieves the best distributional fidelity across all evaluation scenarios, significantly outperforming existing traffic prediction baselines and representative world models. A downstream RL-based case study further validates MobiWM as a simulation environment for network optimization, establishing a new paradigm for digital twin-driven wireless network management.

</details>


### [199] [FORSLICE: An Automated Formal Framework for Efficient PRB-Allocation towards Slicing Multiple Network Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08244)
*Debarpita Banerjee,Sumana Ghosh,Snigdha Das,Shilpa Budhkar,Rana Pratap Sircar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于形式化方法的RAN切片PRB分配方案FORSLICE，通过可满足性模理论建模并验证，在保证公平性和资源最优性的同时，实现了比现有AI方法更可靠的性能保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络切片是5G关键技术，需要高效地将物理资源块（PRB）分配给不同切片。现有基于AI的方法缺乏足够的正确性保证，无法确保一致的系统性能。针对多优先级服务场景，需要在优先分配特定服务的同时保证两个关键属性：各服务类型的公平性和PRB利用率最优性。

Method: 采用三层层次化PRB分区技术，利用形式化方法解决PRB分配问题。具体使用可满足性模理论（SMT）构建FORSLICE框架，对PRB分配问题进行形式化建模，并通过形式化验证确保模型满足公平性和PRB最优性这两个期望的系统属性。

Result: 所提方法具有高效性、通用性和自动化特点，兼容所有三层层次化网络结构配置。与基线方法相比，在公平性和PRB最优性两个核心性能评估指标上取得了显著提升。相比现有AI方法，提供了有保证的正确性和完备性。

Conclusion: 形式化方法能够有效解决网络切片中的PRB分配问题，FORSLICE作为首个保证公平性和最优性的形式化方案，为RAN切片资源管理提供了可靠的技术路径，证明了形式化方法在网络切片资源分配领域的应用价值。

Abstract: Network slicing is a modern 5G technology that provides efficient network experience for diverse use cases. It is a technique for partitioning a single physical network infrastructure into multiple virtual networks, called slices, each equipped for specific services and requirements. In this work, we particularly deal with radio access network (RAN) slicing and resource allocation to RAN slices. In 5G, physical resource blocks (PRBs) being the fundamental units of radio resources, our main focus is to allocate PRBs to the slices efficiently. While addressing a spectrum of needs for multiple services or the same services with multi-priorities, we need to ensure two vital system properties: i) fairness to every service type (i.e., providing the required resources and a desired range of throughput) even after prioritizing a particular service type, and ii) PRB-optimality or minimizing the unused PRBs in slices. These serve as the core performance evaluation metrics for PRB-allocation in our work.
  We adopt the 3-layered hierarchical PRB-partitioning technique for allocating PRBs to network slices. The case-specific, AI-based solution of the state-of-the-art method lacks sufficient correctness to ensure consistent system performance. To achieve guaranteed correctness and completeness, we leverage formal methods and propose the first approach for a fair and optimal PRB distribution to RAN slices. We formally model the PRB-allocation problem as a 3-layered framework, FORSLICE, specifically by employing satisfiability modulo theories. Next, we apply formal verification to ensure that the desired system properties: fairness and PRB-optimality, are satisfied by the model. The proposed method offers an efficient, versatile and automated approach compatible with all 3-layered hierarchical network structure configurations, yielding significant system property improvements compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [200] [Real-Time Cross-Layer Semantic Error Correction Using Language Models and Software-Defined Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08419)
*Yuchen Pan,Yuyang Du,Yirun Wang,Shiqi Xu,Lihao Zhang,Soung Chang Liew*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在实时SDR测试平台上实现了跨层语义错误校正（CL-SEC），通过融合物理层LLR与语义上下文，验证了其在真实环境中显著优于单一信源的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义错误校正方法优先考虑意图保留而非精确恢复，无法实现逐字恢复；作者前期提出的CL-SEC虽解决了此问题，但未在实时系统中验证其可行性。

Method: 在实时软件定义无线电（SDR）测试平台上部署CL-SEC，通过开发SDR中间件实现从FPGA硬件实时提取对数似然比（LLR），并构建通用推理接口以支持现代编码器-解码器语言模型。

Result: 真实环境实验表明，跨层融合方案的性能显著优于单独使用物理层信息或语义上下文的方案。

Conclusion: CL-SEC具备实时部署可行性，跨层融合策略在语义错误校正任务中表现出优越性能，为可靠网络设计提供了有效途径。

Abstract: As Language Models (LMs) advance, Semantic Error Correction (SEC) has emerged as a promising approach for reliable network designs. Yet existing methods prioritize intent over accuracy, falling short of verbatim recovery. Our recent work, Cross-Layer SEC (CL-SEC), addressed this by fusing physical-layer Log-Likelihood Ratios (LLRs) with semantic context, but its real-time feasibility remained unvalidated. This paper demonstrates CL-SEC on a live Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed, resolving implementation barriers with: 1) an SDR middleware enabling real-time LLR extraction from FPGA hardware, and 2) a generalized inference interface supporting modern encoder-decoder LMs. Real-world experiments confirm that the cross-layer fusion significantly outperforms either source alone.

</details>


### [201] [LITE: Lightweight Channel Gain Estimation with Reduced X-Haul CSI Signaling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08458)
*David Goez,Marco Piazzola,Giulia Costa,Achiel Colpaert,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Esra Aycan Beyazit,Nina Slamnik-Krijestorac,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Miguel Camelo Botero*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出LITE轻量级管道，结合1D自编码器压缩和SE-BiLSTM预测，在O-RAN架构中实现高效的CSI预测，显著降低复杂度并提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN中的无蜂窝大规模MIMO系统频谱效率高，但频繁的CSI交换严重消耗X-haul带宽，现有未压缩CSI或重型预测器方案无法满足传输与计算预算约束。

Method: 在O-DU部署1D卷积自编码器进行50% CSI压缩；在Near-RT-RIC部署SE增强的双向LSTM进行短视界、无轨迹感知预测；采用压缩感知训练策略联合优化压缩与预测模块。

Result: 相比BiLSTM基线，模型复杂度降低83.39%，精度提升5%；引入压缩后仅损失6%精度，优于独立与端到端策略；TensorRT优化后吞吐量达147k QPS，提升4.6倍。

Conclusion: LITE实现了X-haul高效、低延迟且部署就绪的信道增益预测，复杂度与精度权衡优异，兼容O-RAN功能分割，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (CF-MaMIMO) in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises high spectral efficiency but is limited by frequent Channel State Information (CSI) exchanges, which strain fronthaul/midhaul/backhaul (X-haul) bandwidth and exceed the capabilities of existing approaches relying on uncompressed CSI or heavy predictors. To overcome these constraints, we propose LITE, a lightweight pipeline combining a 1-D convolutional Autoencoder (AE) at the O-RAN Distributed Unit (O-DU) with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-enhanced Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) predictor at the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT-RIC), enabling short-horizon trajectory-unaware forecasting under strict transport and processing budgets. LITE applies 50% CSI compression and an asymmetric SE-BiLSTM, reducing model complexity by 83.39% while improving accuracy by 5% relative to a baseline BiLSTM. With compression-aware training, the Lightweight Intelligent Trajectory Estimator (LITE) incurs only 6% accuracy loss versus the BiLSTM baseline, outperforming independent and end-to-end strategies. A TensorRT-optimized implementation achieves 147k Queries per Second (QPS), a 4.6x throughput gain. These results demonstrate that LITE delivers X-haul-efficient, low-latency, and deployment-ready channel-gain prediction compatible with O-RAN splits.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Blink: CPU-Free LLM Inference by Delegating the Serving Stack to GPU and SmartNIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07609)
*Mohammad Siavashi,Mariano Scazzariello,Gerald Q. Maguire,Dejan Kostić,Marco Chiesa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Blink通过SmartNIC和GPU协同卸载CPU任务，消除了LLM推理中主机CPU的干扰问题，显著提升了性能和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务栈在推理的关键路径上依赖主机CPU进行编排和token级控制，导致性能对CPU干扰敏感，阻碍应用共存，迫使运营商预留CPU余量，造成资源浪费。

Method: 提出Blink端到端服务架构，将请求处理卸载到SmartNIC（通过RDMA直接传输数据到GPU内存），并用持久化GPU内核替代主机调度，实现无CPU参与的批处理、调度和KV缓存管理。

Result: 相比TensorRT-LLM、vLLM和SGLang，Blink在隔离环境下表现更优：预饱和P99 TTFT降低8.47倍，P99 TPOT降低3.40倍，解码吞吐量提升2.1倍，每token能耗降低48.6%；在CPU干扰下性能保持稳定，而现有系统性能下降高达两个数量级。

Conclusion: Blink成功将主机CPU移出稳态推理路径，有效解决了CPU干扰问题，显著提升了LLM推理的性能、资源利用率和稳定性，为数据中心LLM服务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference is rapidly becoming a core datacenter service, yet current serving stacks keep the host CPU on the critical path for orchestration and token-level control. This makes LLM performance sensitive to CPU interference, undermining application colocation and forcing operators to reserve CPU headroom, leaving substantial capacity unutilized.
  We introduce Blink, an end-to-end serving architecture that removes the host CPU from the steady-state inference path by redistributing responsibilities across a SmartNIC and a GPU. Blink offloads request handling to the SmartNIC, which delivers inputs directly into GPU memory via RDMA, and replaces host-driven scheduling with a persistent GPU kernel that performs batching, scheduling, and KV-cache management without CPU involvement.
  Evaluated against TensorRT-LLM, vLLM, and SGLang, Blink outperforms all baselines even in isolation, reducing pre-saturation P99 TTFT by up to 8.47$\times$ and P99 TPOT by up to 3.40$\times$, improving decode throughput by up to 2.1$\times$, and reducing energy per token by up to 48.6$\%$. Under CPU interference, Blink maintains stable performance, while existing systems degrade by up to two orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [203] [Reduced-Mass Orbital AI Inference via Integrated Solar, Compute, and Radiator Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07760)
*Stephen Gaalema,Samuel Indyk,Clinton Staley*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向SSO计算卫星的分布式架构，集成太阳能电池、散热器和计算功能，实现每吨发射质量>100 kW计算功率和约500 W/kg比功率，单颗卫星可支持16 MW计算和超7900个并发LLM推理会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间计算平台在功率密度、散热效率和载荷质量方面受限，无法满足大规模在轨计算（如大模型推理）的需求；作者希望探索一种高度集成、可扩展、且能在轨道部署的高性能计算卫星架构。

Method: 提出将太阳能电池、蒸汽腔散热器与计算单元集成于多块小型面板，形成大阵列（如20 m × 2200 m，16,000面板），利用散热结构支撑太阳电池以提升比功率；面板尺寸1–4 m²可权衡热传输、计算效率与通信；卫星可整体放入Starship载荷舱或通过在轨组装扩展。

Result: 理论设计可实现>100 kW/吨的计算功率密度，比功率约500 W/kg（现有<100 W/kg）；IC结温可维持约40°C以提升效率与可靠性；以1 kW/面板计，512面板子阵列可运行500k token上下文、128注意力块的推理型LLM，推理速度553 token/秒/会话，支持256个并发会话；整颗卫星可容纳31个子阵列，实现>7900个并发推理。

Conclusion: 该分布式集成架构展示了空间高性能计算卫星的可行路径，具备高功率密度与可扩展性，适合大规模在轨AI推理等应用，后续需通过各组件定制开发与工程验证。

Abstract: We describe and analyze a distributed compute architecture for SSO computational satellites that can potentially provide >100 kW compute power per launched metric ton (including deployment and station keeping mass). The architecture co-locates and integrates the solar cells, radiator, and compute functions into multiple small panels arranged in a large array. The resultant large vapor chamber radiator area per panel should permit ICs to operate at junction temperatures near 40*C with benefits in compute efficiency and reliability. Using the structure of the radiator to support the solar cells may also yield a specific power of about 500 W/kg compared to less than 100 for existing conventional implementations. Assuming development of custom solutions for all components, a 16 MW computation, 150 ton satellite comprising a 20 m x 2200 m grid of 16,000 panels can fit in a single Starship hold. The concept is scalable to much larger satellites with higher mass payloads or using on-orbit assembly. We consider panel sizes from 1 to 4 m2 to allow trading vapor chamber heat transport with compute efficiency and inter-panel communication. Assuming a 1 kW/panel design, 512-panel subarrays of the satellite can run a representative inference-only LLM with 500,000 token context window and 128 attention blocks, at a rate of 553 tokens/sec/session, across 256 simultaneous in-flight sessions. A full satellite could support 31 such subarrays, for >7900 inferences at a time.

</details>


### [204] [Administrative Decentralization in Edge-Cloud Multi-Agent for Mobile Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07767)
*Senyao Li,Zhigang Zuo,Haozhao Wang,Junyu Chen,Zhanbo Jin,Ruixuan LI*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出AdecPilot，通过管理去中心化原则重新定义边缘-云多智能体框架，实现云端高层战略设计与边缘端自主战术执行的解耦，显著提升移动自动化任务成功率并降低延迟和云令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作式边缘-云框架将管理权集中于云端，边缘设备仅被动执行，导致对实时UI动态的认知滞后，无法满足移动自动化对低延迟和实时响应的需求。

Method: 提出AdecPilot框架：1) 基于管理去中心化原则，将高层战略设计（云端UI无关设计器生成抽象里程碑）与战术落地（边缘双模团队自主战术规划与自纠错）解耦；2) 引入分层隐式终止协议（HIT）强制确定性停止，防止任务完成后产生幻觉行为。

Result: 实验表明：相较于EcoAgent，任务成功率提升21.7%，云令牌消耗降低37.5%；相较于CORE，端到端延迟降低88.9%。

Conclusion: AdecPilot通过管理去中心化和层级终止协议有效解决了现有边缘-云框架的认知滞后问题，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了资源消耗和延迟，为移动自动化提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative edge-cloud frameworks have emerged as the main- stream paradigm for mobile automation, mitigating the latency and privacy risks inherent to monolithic cloud agents. However, existing approaches centralize administration in the cloud while relegating the device to passive execution, inducing a cognitive lag regard- ing real-time UI dynamics. To tackle this, we introduce AdecPilot by applying the principle of administrative decentralization to the edge-cloud multi-agent framework, which redefines edge agency by decoupling high-level strategic designing from tactical grounding. AdecPilot integrates a UI-agnostic cloud designer generating ab- stract milestones with a bimodal edge team capable of autonomous tactical planning and self-correction without cloud intervention. Furthermore, AdecPilot employs a Hierarchical Implicit Termi- nation protocol to enforce deterministic stops and prevent post- completion hallucinations. Extensive experiments demonstrate pro- posed approach improves task success rate by 21.7% while reducing cloud token consumption by 37.5% against EcoAgent and decreas- ing end to end latency by 88.9% against CORE. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Anonymous_code- B8AB.

</details>


### [205] [LogAct: Enabling Agentic Reliability via Shared Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07988)
*Mahesh Balakrishnan,Ashwin Bharambe,Davide Testuggine,David Geraghty,David Mao,Vidhya Venkat,Ilya Mironov,Rithesh Baradi,Gayathri Aiyer,Victoria Dudin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LogAct抽象，通过共享日志构建代理状态机，实现执行前可见、可拦截、可一致恢复的代理管理，并支持代理自省能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代理组件能够以强大且任意的方式改变环境，但在生产环境中，由于异步性和故障，难以对其执行提供保障。

Method: 提出LogAct抽象，将每个代理建模为一个基于共享日志的去构化状态机。代理行为在执行前对共享日志可见；可通过可插拔、解耦的投票者在执行前被阻止；在代理或环境故障时能够一致恢复。该机制还支持代理自省，允许代理利用LLM推理分析自身执行历史。

Result: 评估显示LogAct代理能高效且正确地从故障中恢复；能调试自身性能；能在群体中优化token使用；在代表性基准测试中，仅需牺牲3%的良性效用即可阻止目标模型的所有不良行为。

Conclusion: LogAct为生产环境中的代理执行提供了可靠的保障机制，兼具故障恢复、安全控制和性能优化能力。

Abstract: Agents are LLM-driven components that can mutate environments in powerful, arbitrary ways. Extracting guarantees for the execution of agents in production environments can be challenging due to asynchrony and failures. In this paper, we propose a new abstraction called LogAct, where each agent is a deconstructed state machine playing a shared log. In LogAct, agentic actions are visible in the shared log before they are executed; can be stopped prior to execution by pluggable, decoupled voters; and recovered consistently in the case of agent or environment failure. LogAct enables agentic introspection, allowing the agent to analyze its own execution history using LLM inference, which in turn enables semantic variants of recovery, health check, and optimization. In our evaluation, LogAct agents recover efficiently and correctly from failures; debug their own performance; optimize token usage in swarms; and stop all unwanted actions for a target model on a representative benchmark with just a 3% drop in benign utility.

</details>


### [206] [LegoDiffusion: Micro-Serving Text-to-Image Diffusion Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08123)
*Lingyun Yang,Suyi Li,Tianyu Feng,Xiaoxiao Jiang,Zhipeng Di,Weiyi Lu,Kan Liu,Yinghao Yu,Tao Lan,Guodong Yang,Lin Qu,Liping Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LegoDiffusion通过将文本到图像的扩散工作流分解为松耦合的模型执行节点，实现了独立管理和调度，相比现有系统可支持高达3倍的请求率和8倍的突发流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像服务系统将整个扩散工作流视为不透明的整体进行资源管理，掩盖了内部数据流，阻碍了模型共享，并导致资源管理粒度过粗，无法充分利用集群优化潜力。

Method: 提出LegoDiffusion系统，将扩散工作流分解为可独立管理和调度的松耦合模型执行节点。通过显式管理单个模型推理，实现了集群级优化，包括模型级独立伸缩、模型共享和自适应模型并行。

Result: LegoDiffusion在性能上显著优于现有扩散工作流服务系统，能够维持高达3倍的请求率，并容忍高达8倍的突发流量。

Conclusion: 微服务化的扩散工作流架构通过细粒度的模型管理和调度，可以有效提升文本到图像生成系统的性能和弹性，为大规模部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Text-to-image generation executes a diffusion workflow comprising multiple models centered on a base diffusion model. Existing serving systems treat each workflow as an opaque monolith, provisioning, placing, and scaling all constituent models together, which obscures internal dataflow, prevents model sharing, and enforces coarse-grained resource management. In this paper, we make a case for micro-serving diffusion workflows with LegoDiffusion, a system that decomposes a workflow into loosely coupled model-execution nodes that can be independently managed and scheduled. By explicitly managing individual model inference, LegoDiffusion unlocks cluster-scale optimizations, including per-model scaling, model sharing, and adaptive model parallelism. Collectively, LegoDiffusion outperforms existing diffusion workflow serving systems, sustaining up to 3x higher request rates and tolerating up to 8x higher burst traffic.

</details>


### [207] [Wattlytics: A Web Platform for Co-Optimizing Performance, Energy, and TCO in HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08182)
*Ayesha Afzal,Georg Hager,Gerhard Wellein*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 发布了Wattlytics，首个集成GPU性能建模、DVFS功耗分析和TCO总拥有成本分析的交互式决策支持系统，帮助HPC系统设计者在多种约束下做出更优的配置和采购决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU加速计算系统的计算需求不断增长，能源消耗和足迹问题日益突出，使得系统设计和运营决策变得复杂。现有的计算工具主要面向采购，缺乏对性能扩展、功耗建模和长期成本分析的综合支持，无法满足复杂的设计空间探索需求。

Method: 开发了基于浏览器的交互式决策支持系统Wattlytics，集成三大核心功能：(1)基于基准测试的GPU性能扩展建模；(2)感知DVFS的分段功耗建模；(3)多年期TCO总拥有成本分析。支持当代GPU架构（GH200、H100、L40S、L40、A40、A100、L4），允许用户配置异构系统、选择代表性科学工作负载（如GROMACS、AMBER），在能源价格、系统寿命、频率调节等约束下探索部署场景。提供多维决策指标（TCO分解、单位TCO工作量、单位TCO功耗、单位TCO单位瓦特工作量）和敏感性分析（弹性、Sobol指数、蒙特卡洛方法）。

Result: 系统成功实现了设计空间探索、假设场景分析、敏感性分析和协作功能。通过固定预算、固定GPU数量、固定性能和固定功耗四种运营模式的案例研究表明：在预算或能源约束下，优化部署的能效型GPU在整体成本效益上可以超越高性能替代方案。系统能帮助用户区分成本驱动因素和风险驱动因素，区分不同GPU部署策略的优劣。

Conclusion: Wattlytics将HPC系统设计从经验驱动转变为数据驱动的知情决策过程，使用户能够在不确定性条件下探索设计参数空间，识别成本与风险的权衡关系。该工具有助于实现更透明、可解释的集群设计和采购决策，推动HPC系统向更经济、更可持续的方向发展。

Abstract: The escalating computational demands and energy footprint of GPU-accelerated computing systems complicate informed design and operational decisions. We present the first release of Wattlytics (https://wattlytics.netlify.app), an interactive, browser-based decision-support system. Unlike existing procurement-oriented calculators, Wattlytics uniquely integrates benchmark-driven GPU performance scaling, dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS)-aware piecewise power modeling, and multi-year total cost of ownership (TCO) analysis within a single interactive environment. Users can configure heterogeneous systems across contemporary GPU architectures (GH200, H100, L40S, L40, A40, A100, and L4), select representative scientific workloads (e.g., GROMACS, AMBER), and explore deployment scenarios under constraints such as energy prices, system lifetime, and frequency scaling. Wattlytics computes multidimensional decision metrics (TCO breakdown, work-per-TCO, power-per-TCO, and work-per-watt-per-TCO) and supports design-space exploration, what-if scenarios, sensitivity metrics (elasticity, Sobol indices, Monte Carlo) and collaborative features to guide realistic cluster design and procurement under uncertainty. We demonstrate selected scenarios comparing deployment strategies under different operational modes: ixed budget, fixed GPU count, fixed performance, and fixed power. Our case studies show that, under budget or energy constraints, optimally deployed energy-efficient GPUs can outperform higher-performance alternatives in overall cost-effectiveness. Wattlytics helps users explore the design parameter space and distinguish between cost- and risk-driving factors, turning HPC design into a well-informed and explainable decision-making process.

</details>


### [208] [Scheduling Coflows in Multi-Core OCS Networks with Performance Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08242)
*Xin Wang,Hong Shen,Hui Tian,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究多核光电路交换（OCS）网络中的协同流调度问题，提出一种联合跨核流量分配与单核电路调度的近似算法，在保证理论性能界限的同时有效降低了加权协同流完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同流调度研究主要集中于单核网络或多核电分组交换（EPS）网络，缺乏针对多核OCS网络的研究。现代数据中心网络采用多个独立OCS核心并行运行以满足带宽需求，且支持非全局阻塞的重配置模型（单电路重配置不中断其他电路），因此需要针对这一新场景设计调度方案。

Method: 提出一种近似算法框架，联合优化跨核流量分配与各核内电路调度。算法解决两大挑战：（1）异构核心间流量分配产生的跨核耦合；（2）单核OCS调度约束，包括端口独占性和重配置延迟。算法建立了可证明的最坏情况性能保证，并可扩展应用于多核EPS场景。

Result: 在真实Facebook工作负载驱动的仿真实验中，所提算法有效降低了加权协同流完成时间（CCT）和尾端CCT，验证了算法的实际性能。同时，为多核OCS和多核EPS场景分别提供了对应的近似比理论保证。

Conclusion: 本文填补了多核OCS网络协同流调度研究的空白，提出的联合优化算法框架兼具理论保证与实际性能，为现代高带宽需求的数据中心网络提供了有效的调度解决方案。

Abstract: Coflow provides a key application-layer abstraction for capturing communication patterns, enabling the efficient coordination of parallel data flows to reduce job completion times in distributed systems. Modern data center networks (DCNs) are employing multiple independent optical circuit switching (OCS) cores operating concurrently to meet the massive bandwidth demands of application jobs. However, existing coflow scheduling research primarily focuses on the single-core setting, with multi-core fabrics only for EPS (electrical packet switching) networks.
  To address this gap, this paper studies the coflow scheduling problem in multi-core OCS networks under the not-all-stop reconfiguration model in which one circuit's reconfiguration does not interrupt other circuits. The challenges stem from two aspects: (i) cross-core coupling induced by traffic assignment across heterogeneous cores; and (ii) per-core OCS scheduling constraints, namely port exclusivity and reconfiguration delay. We propose an approximation algorithm that jointly integrates cross-core flow assignment and per-core circuit scheduling to minimize the total weighted coflow completion time (CCT) and establish a provable worst-case performance guarantee. Furthermore, our algorithm framework can be directly applied to the multi-core EPS scenario with the corresponding approximation ratio under packet-switched fabrics. Trace-driven simulations using real Facebook workloads demonstrate that our algorithm effectively reduces weighted CCT and tail CCT.

</details>


### [209] [Asynchronous Quantum Distributed Computing: Causality, Snapshots, and Global Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08298)
*Siddhartha Visveswara Jayanti,Anand Natarajan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开创了异步量子分布式系统的研究，提出了一种基于Chandy-Lamport算法的量子分布式算法(QGO)，用于实现可分解为局部操作的原子量子全局操作，证明了即使存在量子纠缠，经典计算因果性在量子世界中依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究异步量子分布式系统，特别是实现可分解为系统组件局部操作的原子量子全局操作。一个简单的例子是量子快照，即对整个系统进行瞬时测量。需要探索经典分布式计算概念（如因果性）在量子环境下是否仍然适用。

Method: 基于Chandy和Lamport的经典快照算法，设计了名为QGO Algorithm的量子分布式算法来实现可分解的全局操作。提出了量子分布式计算的形式化模型，以及全局操作期望行为的形式化规范。利用Lamport的计算因果性概念进行算法分析。

Result: 成功设计了QGO算法来异步实现可分解的量子全局操作。分析表明，基于Lamport计算因果性的论证在量子世界中仍然有效，即使由于纠缠的存在，因果性无法从系统的标准（全局）量子态描述中直接体现。形式化规范在经典设置（如随机算法）中也具有应用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了异步量子分布式系统的基础框架，证明了经典分布式计算的关键概念可以成功迁移到量子领域。尽管量子纠缠使得系统状态描述更为复杂，计算因果性等经典概念仍然保持有效性，为量子分布式计算的理论发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: We initiate the study of asynchronous quantum distributed systems, focusing on the case of implementing atomic quantum global operations that can be decomposed into a collection of local operations on the components of the system. A simple example of such an operation is a quantum snapshot in which the whole system is instantaneously measured. Based on the classical snapshot algorithm of Chandy and Lamport, we design a quantum distributed algorithm to implement such decomposable global operations, which we call the QGO Algorithm. The analysis of our algorithm shows that arguments based on Lamport's computational causality remain valid in the quantum world, even though, due to entanglement, causality is not manifest from the standard description of the system in terms of a (global) quantum state. Our other contributions include a formal model of quantum distributed computing, and a formal specification for the desired behavior of a global operation, which may be of interest even in classical settings (such as in the setting of randomized algorithms).

</details>


### [210] [City-Scale Visibility Graph Analysis via GPU-Accelerated HyperBall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08374)
*Alex Hodge,Melissa Barrientos Trinanes*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套将可视图分析(VGA)扩展至城市尺度的系统，通过压缩存储、HyperBall概率估计和GPU加速三种技术，相比传统工具depthmapX实现了239倍加速，并能处理高达4.8亿条边的图规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGA是理解空间配置如何塑造人类运动的关键空间句法方法，但其依赖全对BFS计算，限制了其在小型研究区域的实际应用，无法扩展到城市尺度问题。

Method: 系统结合三项技术：(i) 使用LEB128变长编码的增量压缩CSR存储，实现约4倍压缩并支持内存映射图；(ii) HyperBall概率距离估计器，基于HyperLogLog计数器传播，首次应用于可视图；(iii) GPU加速CUDA内核，采用融合解码-合并内核在共享内存中完成LEB128解码。

Result: 在42,705个单元上实现239倍端到端加速；扩展至236,000个单元(4.8亿条边)仅需137秒；在p=10时，视觉平均深度达到Pearson r=0.999，中位相对误差1.7%。

Conclusion: 该系统成功突破了depthmapX的实际限制，使VGA能够应用于城市尺度问题，同时保持高精度（r=0.999），为空间句法从业者提供了可扩展的实用工具。

Abstract: Visibility Graph Analysis (VGA) is a key space syntax method for understanding how spatial configuration shapes human movement, but its reliance on all-pairs BFS computation limits practical application to small study areas. We present a system that combines three techniques to scale VGA to city-scale problems: (i) delta-compressed CSR storage using LEB128 varint encoding, which achieves ~4x compression and enables memory-mapped graphs exceeding available RAM; (ii) HyperBall, a probabilistic distance estimator based on HyperLogLog counter propagation, applied here for the first time to visibility graphs, reducing BFS complexity from O(N|E|) to O(D|E|2^p); and (iii) GPU-accelerated CUDA kernels with a fused decode-union kernel that streams the compressed graph via PCIe and performs LEB128 decoding entirely in shared memory. HyperBall's iteration count equals the topological depth limit, so the radius-n analysis that practitioners already use as standard translates directly into proportional speedup -- unlike depthmapX, whose BFS time is invariant to depth setting due to the small diameter of visibility graphs. Using depthmapX's own visibility algorithm (sparkSieve2) to ensure identical edge sets, our tool achieves a 239x end-to-end speedup at 42,705 cells and scales to 236,000 cells (4.8 billion edges) in 137 seconds -- problem sizes far beyond depthmapX's practical limit. At p=10, Visual Mean Depth achieves Pearson r=0.999 with 1.7% median relative error across 20 matched configurations.

</details>


### [211] [NL-CPS: Reinforcement Learning-Based Kubernetes Control Plane Placement in Multi-Region Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08434)
*Sajid Alam,Amjad Ullah,Ze Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经上下文赌博机的强化学习框架，用于在多云边跨区域环境中智能选择Kubernetes控制平面节点的放置位置，实验证明该方法能显著提升集群性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构、跨区域环境中，Kubernetes控制平面节点的放置对集群的可靠性、可扩展性和性能至关重要。现有的初始化程序通常任意选择控制平面主机，未考虑节点资源容量或网络拓扑，导致集群性能次优和弹性降低。鉴于Kubernetes已成为容器编排的事实标准，迫切需要严格评估控制平面节点放置如何影响跨多区域运行集群的整体性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于神经上下文赌博机（Neural Contextual Bandits）的强化学习框架。该框架作为自动化编排系统的一部分，能够观测运行性能，并从基础设施特征中学习最优的控制平面放置策略，从而在跨多个区域的动态云边资源中智能选择控制平面节点的位置。

Result: 在多个地理分布区域和多种集群配置下进行的实验评估表明，与多种基线方法相比，该方法带来了显著的性能改进。

Conclusion: 通过引入智能化的控制平面节点放置方法，能够有效解决传统任意放置策略的不足，为跨区域、异构的云边环境下的Kubernetes集群部署提供了一种可行的自动化优化方案。

Abstract: The placement of Kubernetes control-plane nodes is critical to ensuring cluster reliability, scalability, and performance, and therefore represents a significant deployment challenge in heterogeneous, multi-region environments. Existing initialisation procedures typically select control-plane hosts arbitrarily, without considering node resource capacity or network topology, often leading to suboptimal cluster performance and reduced resilience. Given Kubernetes's status as the de facto standard for container orchestration, there is a need to rigorously evaluate how control-plane node placement influences the overall performance of the cluster operating across multiple regions. This paper advances this goal by introducing an intelligent methodology for selecting control-plane node placement across dynamically selected Cloud-Edge resources spanning multiple regions, as part of an automated orchestration system. More specifically, we propose a reinforcement learning framework based on neural contextual bandits that observes operational performance and learns optimal control-plane placement policies from infrastructure characteristics. Experimental evaluation across several geographically distributed regions and multiple cluster configurations demonstrates substantial performance improvements over several baseline approaches.

</details>


### [212] [Taming GPU Underutilization via Static Partitioning and Fine-grained CPU Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08451)
*Gabin Schieffer,Ruimin Shi,Jie Ren,Ivy Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对GPU共享技术（特别是MIG）在HPC工作负载中的效果进行了系统级评估，发现虽然能提高资源利用率但存在干扰问题，并提出了基于Nvlink-C2C的内存卸载方案来解决资源粒度不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU计算能力和内存容量的提升，如何高效利用这些资源仍面临挑战。不同应用特性导致资源利用不均衡。MIG通过将GPU资源划分为固定大小的隔离切片来提高利用率，但其在支持HPC工作负载方面的有效性和局限性尚不明确。

Method: 使用真实世界的科学计算、AI和数据分析应用（包括NekRS、LAMMPS、Llama3和Qiskit）对不同GPU共享选项进行全面的系统级特征分析。通过性能-资源扩展实验评估粗粒度资源分配与应用需求的匹配程度，并提出利用缓存一致性Nvlink-C2C互连的内存卸载方案。

Result: 通过MIG进行的GPU共享可以显著减少资源利用不足，并在系统层面提升吞吐量和能效。然而，共享资源（如功率节流）仍会导致干扰。性能-资源扩展结果表明，紧耦合的计算和内存资源的粗粒度配置通常与应用需求不匹配。

Conclusion: GPU共享技术虽有潜力但存在局限性。所提出的内存卸载方案能够弥合粗粒度资源切片与应用需求之间的差距，减少资源利用不足问题。该方案利用Nvlink-C2C互连的缓存一致性特性来优化资源分配。

Abstract: Advances in GPU compute throughput and memory capacity brings significant opportunities to a wide range of workloads. However, efficiently utilizing these resources remains challenging, particularly because diverse application characteristics may result in imbalanced utilization. Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) is a promising approach to improve utilization by partitioning GPU compute and memory resources into fixed-size slices with isolation. Yet, its effectiveness and limitations in supporting HPC workloads remain an open question. We present a comprehensive system-level characterization of different GPU sharing options using real-world scientific, AI, and data analytics applications, including NekRS, LAMMPS, Llama3, and Qiskit. Our analysis reveals that while GPU sharing via MIG can significantly reduce resource underutilization, and enable system-level improvements in throughput and energy, interference still occurs through shared resources, such as power throttling. Our performance-resource scaling results indicate that coarse-grained provisioning for tightly coupled compute and memory resources often mismatches application needs. To address this mismatch, we propose a memory-offloading scheme that leverages the cache-coherent Nvlink-C2C interconnect to bridge the gap between coarse-grained resource slices and reduce resource underutilization.

</details>
